class PasswordHash {\n    static final int SALT_BYTES = 16;\n    static final int HASH_BYTES = 32;\n    static final int MIN_ITERATIONS = 1_000;\n    static final int MAX_ITERATIONS = 5_000_000;\n    private static final String ALGORITHM = \"PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256\";\n    private static final Base64.Encoder ENCODER = Base64.getEncoder();\n    private static final Base64.Decoder DECODER = Base64.getDecoder();\n\n    private PasswordHash() {}\n\n    static byte[] newSalt(SecureRandom random) { byte[] s = new byte[SALT_BYTES]; random.nextBytes(s); return s; }\n\n    static byte[] derive(char[] password, byte[] salt, int iterations) {\n        if (password == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"password missing\");\n        if (salt == null || salt.length != SALT_BYTES) throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"salt must be \" + SALT_BYTES + \" bytes\");\n        if (iterations < MIN_ITERATIONS || iterations > MAX_ITERATIONS) throw new IllegalArgumentException(...);\n        PBEKeySpec spec = new PBEKeySpec(password, salt, iterations, HASH_BYTES * 8);\n        try {\n            byte[] out = SecretKeyFactory.getInstance(ALGORITHM).generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();\n            if (out.length != HASH_BYTES) throw new IllegalStateException(\"unexpected digest length \" + out.length);\n            return out;\n        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException | InvalidKeySpecException e) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"password hashing unavailable: \" + e.getMessage(), e);\n        } finally { spec.clearPassword(); }\n    }\n\n    static boolean matches(char[] candidate, byte[] salt, byte[] storedHash, int iterations) {\n        // Always derive, then compare in constant time.\n        byte[] derived = derive(candidate, salt, iterations);\n        return MessageDigest.isEqual(derived, storedHash);\n    }\n\n    static String encode(byte[] raw) { return ENCODER.encodeToString(raw); }\n\n    static byte[] decodeCanonical(String text, int expectedLength, String what) {\n        if (text == null) return null;\n        byte[] raw;\n        try { raw = DECODER.decode(text); } catch (IllegalArgumentException notBase64) { return null; }\n        // The decoder accepts unpadded input, so re-encode to reject anything\n        // that is not the canonical spelling of those bytes.\n        if (!ENCODER.encodeToString(raw).equals(text)) return null;\n        if (raw.length != expectedLength) return null;\n        return raw;\n    }\n}\n```\nNeed `java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException` import.\n\nUsernameRules:\n```java\nfinal class UsernameRules {\n    static final int MIN_LENGTH = 3;\n    static final int MAX_LENGTH = 64;\n    private UsernameRules() {}\n\n    static Optional<String> normalize(String raw) {\n        if (raw == null) return Optional.empty();\n        int from = 0; int to = raw.length();\n        // Only spaces are trimmed. String.trim() also deletes control characters,\n        // which would fold \"alice\" + U+0000 onto the real account name.\n        while (from < to && raw.charAt(from) == ' ') from++;\n        while (to > from && raw.charAt(to - 1) == ' ') to--;\n        if (to - from < MIN_LENGTH || to - from > MAX_LENGTH) return Optional.empty();\n        char[] folded = new char[to - from];\n        for (int i = from; i < to; i++) {\n            char c = raw.charAt(i);\n            if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') { folded[i - from] = c; }\n            else if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') { folded[i-from] = (char)(c + 32); }\n            else if ((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-') { folded[i-from] = c; }\n            else return Optional.empty();  // covers space, control chars, DEL, non-ASCII\n        }\n        if (!Character.isLetterOrDigit(folded[0]) || !Character.isLetterOrDigit(folded[folded.length-1])) return Optional.empty();\n        return Optional.of(new String(folded));\n    }\n}\n```\nNon-ASCII chars > 0x7F → rejected by the whitelist. Good, no Locale needed.\n\nPasswordRules:\n```java\nfinal class PasswordRules {\n    static final int MIN_LOGIN_LENGTH = 1;\n    static final int MAX_LENGTH = 1024;\n    static final int CREATION_MIN_LENGTH = 10;\n    private PasswordRules() {}\n\n    static Optional<String> structuralProblem(char[] password) {\n        if (password == null || password.length < MIN_LOGIN_LENGTH) return Optional.of(\"password missing\");\n        if (password.length > MAX_LENGTH) return Optional.of(\"password too long\");\n        for (int i = 0; i < password.length; i++) {\n            char c = password[i];\n            if (c < 0x20 || c == 0x7F) return Optional.of(\"password contains a control character\");\n            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c)) {\n                if (i + 1 >= password.length || !Character.isLowSurrogate(password[i+1])) return Optional.of(\"password contains an unpaired surrogate\");\n                i++;\n            } else if (Character.isLowSurrogate(c)) {\n                return Optional.of(\"password contains an unpaired surrogate\");\n            }\n        }\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n\n    static Optional<String> creationProblem(char[] password) {\n        Optional<String> structural = structuralProblem(password);\n        if (structural.isPresent()) return structural;\n        if (password.length < CREATION_MIN_LENGTH) return Optional.of(\"password must be at least \" + CREATION_MIN_LENGTH + \" characters\");\n        boolean letter = false, digit = false;\n        for (char c : password) { if (Character.isLetter(c)) letter = true; else if (Character.isDigit(c)) digit = true; }\n        if (!letter || !digit) return Optional.of(\"password must contain at least one letter and one digit\");\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n}\n```\nWhy structural matters: PBEKeySpec drops trailing NULs and maps unpaired surrogates to '?', so two different char[] could hash the same.\n\nEmployeeRecord:\n```java\nrecord EmployeeRecord(String userName, String salt, String hash, int iterations, AccountStatus status) {\n    EmployeeRecord {\n        Objects.requireNonNull(userName, ...);\n    }\n    boolean wellFormed() {\n        return UsernameRules.normalize(userName).map(userName::equals).orElse(false)   // already normalized\n            && PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(salt, SALT_BYTES, \"salt\") != null\n            && PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(hash, HASH_BYTES, \"hash\") != null\n            && iterations in range;\n    }\n    EmployeeRecord withStatus(AccountStatus s) { return new EmployeeRecord(userName, salt, hash, iterations, s); }\n\n    String hash() {\n        return hash;\n    }\n\n    int iterations() {\n        return iterations;\n    }\n\n    AccountStatus status() {\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    EmployeeRecord     if (iterations < PasswordHash.MIN_ITERATIONS || iterations > PasswordHash.MAX_ITERATIONS) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if              && PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(hash, PasswordHash.HASH_BYTES) != null;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return \"EmployeeRecord[\" + userName + \", iterations=\" + iterations + \", status=\" + status + \"]\";\n    }\n}\n\n/** Whether an account is allowed to sign in at all. */\nenum AccountStatus {\n\n    ACTIVE,\n    DISABLED;\n\n    static Optional<AccountStatus> parse(String text) {\n        for (AccountStatus candidate : values()) {\n            if (candidate.name().equalsIgnoreCase(text)) {\n                return Optional.of(candidate);\n            }\n        }\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * A plain text file of records, one per line:\n *\n *     username|salt|hash|iterations|status\n *\n * Blank lines and lines that start with '#' are comments. A line that does not parse is left\n * alone in the file and reported as malformed rather than silently dropped, so a typo cannot\n * quietly turn into an account that accepts a different password. Rewrites go through a\n * temporary file and an atomic move, with owner-only permissions.\n */\nfinal class FileRecordStore {\n\n    static final String HEADER = \"# employee-portal records v1: username|salt|hash|iterations|status\";\n\n    private final Path path;\n    private final Map<String, EmployeeRecord> records = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n    private int malformedLines;\n    private int duplicateLines;\n\n    FileRecordStore(Path path) throws IOException {\n        this.path = path;\n        if (Files.exists(path)) {\n            reload();\n        }\n    }\n\n    Path path() {\n        return path;\n    }\n\n    synchronized int size() {\n        return records.size();\n    }\n\n    synchronized int malformedLineCount() {\n        return malformedLines;\n    }\n\n    synchronized int duplicateLineCount() {\n        return duplicateLines;\n    }\n\n    synchronized List<String> names() {\n        return new ArrayList<>(records.keySet());\n    }\n\n    synchronized Optional<EmployeeRecord> find(String normalizedUserName) {\n        return Optional.ofNullable(records.get(normalizedUserName));\n    }\n\n    /** Inserts or replaces a record and flushes the whole store to disk. */\n    synchronized void put(EmployeeRecord record) throws IOException {\n        if (!record.isWellFormed()) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"refusing to store a malformed record for \"\n                    + record.userName());\n        }\n        records.put(record.userName(), record);\n        flush();\n    }\n\n    /** Adds a record only when the username is free. Returns false when it is taken. */\n    synchronized boolean createIfAbsent(EmployeeRecord record) throws IOException {\n        if (records.containsKey(record.userName())) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        put(record);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private void reload() throws IOException {\n        records.clear();\n        malformedLines = 0;\n        duplicateLines = 0;\n        for (String line : Files.readAllLines(path, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)) {\n            String trimmed = line.trim();\n            if (trimmed.isEmpty() || trimmed.startsWith(\"#\")) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String[] field = trimmed.split(\"\\\\|\", -1);\n            if (field.length != 5) {\n                malformedLines++;\n                continue;\n            }\n            String name = UsernameRules.normalize(field[0]).orElse(null);\n            if (name == null || !name.equals(field[0])) {\n                malformedLines++;\n                continue;\n            }\n            int iterations;\n            try {\n                iterations = Integer.parseInt(field[3]);\n            } catch (NumberFormatException notANumber) {\n                malformedLines++;\n                continue;\n            }\n            AccountStatus status = AccountStatus.parse(field[4]).orElse(null);\n            if (status == null) {\n                malformedLines++;\n                continue;\n            }\n            EmployeeRecord candidate =\n                    new EmployeeRecord(name, field[1], field[2], iterations, status);\n            if (!candidate.isWellFormed()) {\n                malformedLines++;\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (records.containsKey(name)) {\n                duplicateLines++;\n                continue;\n            }\n            records.put(name, candidate);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void flush() throws IOException {\n        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>(records.size() + 1);\n        lines.add(HEADER);\n        for (EmployeeRecord record : records.values()) {\n            lines.add(String.join(\"|\", record.userName(), record.salt(), record.hash(),\n                    Integer.toString(record.iterations()), record.status().name()));\n        }\n        Path parent = path.toAbsolutePath().getParent();\n        Files.createDirectories(parent);\n        Path temp = Files.createTempFile(parent, path.getFileName().toString(), \".tmp\");\n        try {\n            Files.write(temp, lines, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n            restrictToOwner(temp);\n            try {\n                Files.move(temp, path, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING,\n                        StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE);\n            } catch (AtomicMoveNotSupportedException platformWithoutAtomicMove) {\n                Files.move(temp, path, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);\n            }\n        } finally {\n            Files.deleteIfExists(temp);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void restrictToOwner(Path file) {\n        try {\n            Files.setPosixFilePermissions(file, PosixFilePermissions.fromString(\"rw-------\"));\n        } catch (UnsupportedOperationException | IOException notPosix) {\n            // Best effort: a file system without POSIX permissions keeps its defaults.\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Turns salt and password bytes into a digest, and back again.\n *\n * The base64 helpers are strict on purpose: {@link Base64.Decoder} happily accepts unpadded\n * input and ignores nothing else, so a stored digest is only accepted when re-encoding it\n * reproduces the exact text that was decoded.\n */\nfinal class PasswordHash {\n\n    static final int SALT_BYTES = 16;\n    static final int HASH_BYTES = 32;\n    static final int MIN_ITERATIONS = 1_000;\n    static final int MAX_ITERATIONS = 5_000_000;\n\n    private static final String ALGORITHM = \"PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256\";\n    private static final Base64.Encoder BASE64_ENCODER = Base64.getEncoder();\n    private static final Base64.Decoder BASE64_DECODER = Base64.getDecoder();\n\n    private PasswordHash() {\n    }\n\n    static byte[] newSalt(SecureRandom random) {\n        byte[] salt = new byte[SALT_BYTES];\n        random.nextBytes(salt);\n        return salt;\n    }\n\n    /** Derives the digest. Throws if this JVM lacks PBKDF2 or the inputs are out of range. */\n    static byte[] derive(char[] password, byte[] salt, int iterations) {\n        if (password == null) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"no password to hash\");\n        }\n        if (salt == null || salt.length != SALT_BYTES) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"salt must be exactly \" + SALT_BYTES + \" bytes\");\n        }\n        if (iterations < MIN_ITERATIONS || iterations > MAX_ITERATIONS) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"iterations must be between \" + MIN_ITERATIONS\n                    + \" and \" + MAX_ITERATIONS);\n        }\n        PBEKeySpec spec = new PBEKeySpec(password, salt, iterations, HASH_BYTES * 8);\n        try {\n            byte[] digest = SecretKeyFactory.getInstance(ALGORITHM).generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();\n            if (digest.length != HASH_BYTES) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"expected \" + HASH_BYTES + \" digest bytes, got \"\n                        + digest.length);\n            }\n            return digest;\n        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException | InvalidKeySpecException unavailable) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(ALGORITHM + \" is not usable here: \"\n                    + unavailable.getMessage(), unavailable);\n        } finally {\n            spec.clearPassword();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Compares a candidate password with a stored digest without leaking the digest. */\n    static boolean matches(char[] candidate, byte[] salt, byte[] storedDigest, int iterations) {\n        return MessageDigest.isEqual(derive(candidate, salt, iterations), storedDigest);\n    }\n\n    static String encode(byte[] raw) {\n        return BASE64_ENCODER.encodeToString(raw);\n    }\n\n    /** Returns the bytes, or null when the text is not the canonical base64 of that many bytes. */\n    static byte[] decodeCanonical(String text, int expectedBytes) {\n        if (text == null || text.isEmpty()) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        byte[] raw;\n        try {\n            raw = BASE64_DECODER.decode(text);\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException notBase64) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        if (!BASE64_ENCODER.encodeToString(raw).equals(text) || raw.length != expectedBytes) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        return raw;\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Username syntax and the canonical form used to look an account up.\n *\n * Only plain spaces are stripped from the ends. {@code String.trim()} also deletes control\n * characters, which would fold \"alice\" followed by an invisible NUL onto the real account, so\n * this class strips spaces and then requires every remaining character to be on the whitelist.\n */\nfinal class UsernameRules {\n\n    static final int MIN_LENGTH = 3;\n    static final int MAX_LENGTH = 64;\n\n    private UsernameRules() {\n    }\n\n    /** Returns the lower-case canonical name, or empty when the input is not a usable name. */\n    static Optional<String> normalize(String raw) {\n        if (raw == null) {\n            return Optional.empty();\n        }\n        int from = 0;\n        int to = raw.length();\n        while (from < to && raw.charAt(from) == ' ') {\n            from++;\n        }\n        while (to > from && raw.charAt(to - 1) == ' ') {\n            to--;\n        }\n        int length = to - from;\n        if (length < MIN_LENGTH || length > MAX_LENGTH) {\n            return Optional.empty();\n        }\n        char[] canonical = new char[length];\n        for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {\n            char c = raw.charAt(from + i);\n            if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') {\n                canonical[i] = c;\n            } else if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') {\n                canonical[i] = (char) (c - 'A' + 'a');\n            } else if ((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-') {\n                canonical[i] = c;\n            } else {\n                // Rejects spaces, tabs, newlines, NUL, escapes, accented letters, everything.\n                return Optional.empty();\n            }\n        }\n        if (!isLetterOrDigit(canonical[0]) || !isLetterOrDigit(canonical[length - 1])) {\n            return Optional.empty();\n        }\n        return Optional.of(new String(canonical));\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isLetterOrDigit(char c) {\n        return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9');\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Password sanity checks, run before anything is hashed.\n *\n * {@link PBEKeySpec} is not a faithful copy of the characters it is given: it drops trailing\n * NULs and turns an unpaired surrogate into '?'. Two different inputs would then hash to the\n * same value, so control characters and lone surrogates are refused up front.\n */\nfinal class PasswordRules {\n\n    static final int MAX_LENGTH = 1_024;\n    static final int CREATION_MIN_LENGTH = 10;\n\n    private PasswordRules() {\n    }\n\n    /** Empty when the characters can be hashed faithfully. */\n    static Optional<String> structuralProblem(char[] password) {\n        if (password == null || password.length == 0) {\n            return Optional.of(\"no password entered\");\n        }\n        if (password.length > MAX_LENGTH) {\n            return Optional.of(\"password longer than \" + MAX_LENGTH + \" characters\");\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < password.length; i++) {\n            char c = password[i];\n            if (c < 0x20 || c == 0x7F) {\n                return Optional.of(\"password contains a control character\");\n            }\n            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c)) {\n                if (i + 1 >= password.length || !Character.isLowSurrogate(password[i + 1])) {\n                    return Optional.of(\"password contains half of a surrogate pair\");\n                }\n                i++;\n            } else if (Character.isLowSurrogate(c)) {\n                return Optional.of(\"password contains half of a surrogate pair\");\n            }\n        }\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n\n    /** Structural checks plus the minimum strength asked of a brand new password. */\n    static Optional<String> creationProblem(char[] password) {\n        Optional<String> problem = structuralProblem(password);\n        if (problem.isPresent()) {\n            return problem;\n        }\n        if (password.length < CREATION_MIN_LENGTH) {\n            return Optional.of(\"a new password needs at least \" + CREATION_MIN_LENGTH + \" characters\");\n        }\n        boolean hasLetter = false;\n        boolean hasDigit = false;\n        for (char c : password) {\n            hasLetter |= Character.isLetter(c);\n            hasDigit |= Character.isDigit(c);\n        }\n        if (!hasLetter || !hasDigit) {\n            return Optional.of(\"a new password needs at least one letter and one digit\");\n        }\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n}\n\n/** Counted failed logins and the temporary lock that follows them. */\nfinal class LoginAttemptTracker {\n\n    enum Phase {\n        OPEN,\n        LOCKED\n    }\n\n    record Inspection(Phase phase, int failures, Instant lockedUntil) {\n\n        static Inspection open() {\n            return new Inspection(Phase.OPEN, 0, null);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static final class State {\n\n        private int failures;\n        private Instant lockedUntil;\n    }\n\n    private final int maxFailures;\n    private final Duration lockDuration;\n    private final Map<String, State> states = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n\n    LoginAttemptTracker(int maxFailures, Duration lockDuration) {\n        this.maxFailures = maxFailures;\n        this.lockDuration = lockDuration;\n    }\n\n    synchronized Inspection inspect(String userName, Instant now) {\n        State state = states.get(userName);\n        if (state == null) {\n            return Inspection.open();\n        }\n        if (state.lockedUntil != null) {\n            if (now.isBefore(state.lockedUntil)) {\n                return new Inspection(Phase.LOCKED, state.failures, state.lockedUntil);\n            }\n            // The lock has expired, so start the count over rather than resume it.\n            states.remove(userName);\n            return Inspection.open();\n        }\n        return new Inspection(Phase.OPEN, state.failures, null);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Records one failed login.\n     *\n     * @return true when this particular failure is the one that closed the account\n     */\n    synchronized boolean registerFailure(String userName, Instant now) {\n        if (inspect(userName, now).phase() == Phase.LOCKED) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        State state = states.computeIfAbsent(userName, key -> new State());\n        state.failures++;\n        if (state.failures >= maxFailures) {\n            state.lockedUntil = now.plus(lockDuration);\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    synchronized void registerSuccess(String userName) {\n        states.remove(userName);\n    }\n\n    synchronized void unlock(String userName) {\n        states.remove(userName);\n    }\n\n    synchronized int failures(String userName) {\n        return inspect(userName, Instant.now()).failures();\n    }\n\n    synchronized Duration remainingLock(String userName, Instant now) {\n        Inspection inspection = inspect(userName, now);\n        if (inspection.phase() != Phase.LOCKED) {\n            return Duration.ZERO;\n        }\n        Duration remaining = Duration.between(now, inspection.lockedUntil());\n        return remaining.isNegative() ? Duration.ZERO : remaining;\n    }\n}\n\n/** A login that went through: who it belongs to, when it was issued and when it lapses. */\nrecord AccessSession(String token, String userName, Instant issuedAt, Instant expiresAt) {\n\n    boolean isCurrentAt(Instant now) {\n        return now.isBefore(expiresAt);\n    }\n\n    /** A short handle for the log; the full token stays out of the log. */\n    String fingerprint() {\n        return token.length() <= 8 ? token : token.substring(0, 8);\n    }\n}\n\n/** Issues and checks access sessions for logins that were granted. */\nfinal class SessionRegistry {\n\n    private static final Base64.Encoder TOKEN_ENCODER = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding();\n    private static final int TOKEN_BYTES = 32;\n\n    private final Duration timeToLive;\n    private final SecureRandom random;\n    private final TimeSource time;\n    private final Map<String, AccessSession> live = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n\n    SessionRegistry(Duration timeToLive, SecureRandom random, TimeSource time) {\n        this.timeToLive = timeToLive;\n        this.random = random;\n        this.time = time;\n    }\n\n    synchronized AccessSession issue(String userName) {\n        byte[] raw = new byte[TOKEN_BYTES];\n        random.nextBytes(raw);\n        Instant now = time.now();\n        AccessSession session =\n                new AccessSession(TOKEN_ENCODER.encodeToString(raw), userName, now, now.plus(timeToLive));\n        live.put(session.token(), session);\n        return session;\n    }\n\n    /** Returns the session only while its token is valid and unexpired. */\n    synchronized Optional<AccessSession> validate(String token, Instant now) {\n        AccessSession session = live.get(token);\n        if (session == null) {\n            return Optional.empty();\n        }\n        if (!session.isCurrentAt(now)) {\n            live.remove(token);\n            return Optional.empty();\n        }\n        return Optional.of(session);\n    }\n\n    synchronized boolean revoke(String token) {\n        return live.remove(token) != null;\n    }\n\n    synchronized int activeCount() {\n        return live.size();\n    }\n}\n\n/** The decision made about one login attempt. */\nfinal class LoginOutcome {\n\n    enum Status {\n        /** Credentials matched and an access session was issued. */\n        GRANTED,\n        /** Wrong username or wrong password, or input that is not a possible credential. */\n        DENIED,\n        /** Too many earlier failures on this account; try again once the lock lapses. */\n        LOCKED,\n        /** The account is on file but switched off by an operator. */\n        UNAVAILABLE\n    }\n\n    private final Status status;\n    private final String auditReason;\n    private final String userName;\n    private final AccessSession session;\n    private final Duration lockRemaining;\n\n    private LoginOutcome(Status status, String auditReason, String userName, AccessSession session,\n                         Duration lockRemaining) {\n        this.status = status;\n        this.auditReason = auditReason;\n        this.userName = userName;\n        this.session = session;\n        this.lockRemaining = lockRemaining;\n    }\n\n    static LoginOutcome granted(String userName, AccessSession session) {\n        return new LoginOutcome(Status.GRANTED, \"ok\", userName, session, Duration.ZERO);\n    }\n\n    static LoginOutcome denied(String auditReason) {\n        return new LoginOutcome(Status.DENIED, auditReason, null, null, Duration.ZERO);\n    }\n\n    static LoginOutcome locked(Duration remaining) {\n        return new LoginOutcome(Status.LOCKED, \"account-locked\", null, null, remaining);\n    }\n\n    static LoginOutcome unavailable(String auditReason) {\n        return new LoginOutcome(Status.UNAVAILABLE, auditReason, null, null, Duration.ZERO);\n    }\n\n    Status status() {\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    /** Internal cause, meant for the log line rather than for the person typing. */\n    String auditReason() {\n        return auditReason;\n    }\n\n    /** The signed-in name, or null unless the attempt was granted. */\n    String userName() {\n        return userName;\n    }\n\n    AccessSession session() {\n        return session;\n    }\n\n    Duration lockRemaining() {\n        return lockRemaining;\n    }\n\n    long lockRemainingMinutes() {\n        long seconds = lockRemaining.getSeconds();\n        return seconds <= 0 ? 1 : (seconds + 59) / 60;\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Decides whether a username and password combination may use the portal.\n *\n * Every path through {@link #login} performs exactly one PBKDF2 derivation. Unknown usernames,\n * malformed usernames and locked accounts are all answered at the same cost as a real\n * credential check, which is what stops response time from announcing which names exist.\n */\nfinal class PortalAuthService {\n\n    /** Hashed in place of a real candidate when there is no account to check against. */\n    private static final char[] PROBE_PASSWORD =\n            \"portal-timing-probe-passphrase\".toCharArray();\n\n    private final FileRecordStore store;\n    private final LoginAttemptTracker tracker;\n    private final SessionRegistry sessions;\n    private final AuditLog audit;\n    private final SecureRandom random;\n    private final int policyIterations;\n    private final TimeSource time;\n    private final byte[] probeSalt;\n    private final byte[] probeDigest;\n\n    PortalAuthService(FileRecordStore store, LoginAttemptTracker tracker, SessionRegistry sessions,\n                      AuditLog audit, SecureRandom random, int policyIterations, TimeSource time) {\n        this.store = store;\n        this.tracker = tracker;\n        this.sessions = sessions;\n        this.audit = audit;\n        this.random = random;\n        this.policyIterations = policyIterations;\n        this.time = time;\n        this.probeSalt = PasswordHash.newSalt(random);\n        this.probeDigest = PasswordHash.derive(PROBE_PASSWORD, probeSalt, policyIterations);\n        // A probe digest that quietly failed to compute would make every unknown username\n        // return instantly, so prove the probe works before serving a single login.\n        if (!PasswordHash.matches(PROBE_PASSWORD, probeSalt, probeDigest, policyIterations)\n                || PasswordHash.matches(\"not-the-probe-passphrase\".toCharArray(), probeSalt,\n                        probeDigest, policyIterations)) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"the timing probe does not behave as expected\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks one login attempt. The password array is the caller's: this method never clears or\n     * rewrites it, so the caller stays free to use it (the console wipes it when done).\n     */\n    LoginOutcome login(String rawUserName, char[] password) {\n        Instant now = time.now();\n        Optional<String> canonicalName = UsernameRules.normalize(rawUserName);\n        Optional<String> passwordProblem = PasswordRules.structuralProblem(password);\n        if (canonicalName.isEmpty() || passwordProblem.isPresent()) {\n            burnOneDerivation(PROBE_PASSWORD, probeSalt, probeDigest);\n            audit.record(\"login-rejected\", canonicalName.orElse(\"<unusable-name>\"),\n                    passwordProblem.orElse(\"unusable username\"));\n            return LoginOutcome.denied(passwordProblem.isPresent()\n                    ? passwordProblem.get() : \"unusable username\");\n        }\n\n        String userName = canonicalName.get();\n        EmployeeRecord record = store.find(userName).orElse(null);\n        if (record == null) {\n            burnOneDerivation(PROBE_PASSWORD, probeSalt, probeDigest);\n            audit.record(\"login-denied\", userName, \"no such account\");\n            return LoginOutcome.denied(\"no such account\");\n        }\n\n        byte[] salt = PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(record.salt(), PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES);\n        byte[] digest = PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(record.hash(), PasswordHash.HASH_BYTES);\n        // isWellFormed() already proved both decode, so a null here means the record was built\n        // somewhere other than the store. Treat it as a bad account rather than trusting it.\n        if (salt == null || digest == null) {\n            burnOneDerivation(PROBE_PASSWORD, probeSalt, probeDigest);\n            audit.record(\"login-denied\", userName, \"record on file is unreadable\");\n            return LoginOutcome.unavailable(\"record on file is unreadable\");\n        }\n\n        boolean credentialsMatch = PasswordHash.matches(password, salt, digest, record.iterations());\n        LoginAttemptTracker.Inspection before = tracker.inspect(userName, now);\n        if (record.status() == AccountStatus.DISABLED) {\n            audit.record(\"login-denied\", userName, \"account disabled\");\n            return LoginOutcome.unavailable(\"account disabled\");\n        }\n        if (before.phase() == LoginAttemptTracker.Phase.LOCKED) {\n            Duration remaining = tracker.remainingLock(userName, now);\n            audit.record(\"login-refused\", userName,\n                    \"locked for \" + remaining.getSeconds() + \" more second(s)\");\n            return LoginOutcome.locked(remaining);\n        }\n        if (!credentialsMatch) {\n            boolean trippedLock = tracker.registerFailure(userName, now);\n            audit.record(\"login-denied\", userName,\n                    trippedLock ? \"wrong password, account now locked\" : \"wrong password\");\n            return LoginOutcome.denied(\"wrong password\");\n        }\n\n        tracker.registerSuccess(userName);\n        if (record.iterations() < policyIterations) {\n            raiseCost(userName, password, record);\n        }\n        AccessSession session = sessions.issue(userName);\n        audit.record(\"login-granted\", userName, \"session \" + session.fingerprint());\n        return LoginOutcome.granted(userName, session);\n    }\n\n    /** Accounts are switched off by an operator, so count nothing and say the account is off. */\n    private void burnOneDerivation(char[] candidate, byte[] salt, byte[] digest) {\n        PasswordHash.matches(candidate, salt, digest, policyIterations);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * When an account was created with a cheaper setting than the portal now requires, and the\n     * plain password happens to be in hand, re-hash it at today's cost while we can.\n     */\n    private void raiseCost(String userName, char[] password, EmployeeRecord record) {\n        EmployeeRecord raised = record.withIterations(policyIterations);\n        try {\n            char[] copy = password.clone();\n            raised = new EmployeeRecord(userName, record.salt(),\n                    PasswordHash.encode(PasswordHash.derive(copy, PasswordHash\n                            .decodeCanonical(record.salt(), PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES), policyIterations)),\n                    policyIterations, record.status());\n            Arrays.fill(copy, ' ');\n            store.put(raised);\n            audit.record(\"password-cost-raised\", userName,\n                    record.iterations() + \" -> \" + policyIterations + \" iterations\");\n        } catch (IOException couldNotWrite) {\n            audit.record(\"password-cost-not-raised\", userName, couldNotWrite.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Creates a new account, or returns the reason it was refused. */\n    Optional<String> createAccount(String rawUserName, char[] password) {\n        Optional<String> canonicalName = UsernameRules.normalize(rawUserName);\n        if (canonicalName.isEmpty()) {\n            return Optional.of(\"username must be \" + UsernameRules.MIN_LENGTH + \"-\"\n                    + UsernameRules.MAX_LENGTH + \" letters, digits, dot, underscore or hyphen\");\n        }\n        Optional<String> problem = PasswordRules.creationProblem(password);\n        if (problem.isPresent()) {\n            return problem;\n        }\n        byte[] salt = PasswordHash.newSalt(random);\n        EmployeeRecord record = new EmployeeRecord(canonicalName.get(), PasswordHash.encode(salt),\n                PasswordHash.encode(PasswordHash.derive(password, salt, policyIterations)),\n                policyIterations, AccountStatus.ACTIVE);\n        try {\n            if (!store.createIfAbsent(record)) {\n                return Optional.of(\"an account named \" + canonicalName.get() + \" already exists\");\n            }\n        } catch (IOException couldNotWrite) {\n            return Optional.of(\"could not write the records store: \" + couldNotWrite.getMessage());\n        }\n        audit.record(\"account-created\", canonicalName.get(), \"cost \" + policyIterations);\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n\n    /** Turns an account's ability to sign in on or off. */\n    Optional<String> setStatus(String rawUserName, AccountStatus status) {\n        Optional<String> canonicalName = UsernameRules.normalize(rawUserName);\n        if (canonicalName.isEmpty()) {\n            return Optional.of(\"that is not a usable username\");\n        }\n        EmployeeRecord record = store.find(canonicalName.get()).orElse(null);\n        if (record == null) {\n            return Optional.of(\"no account named \" + canonicalName.get());\n        }\n        try {\n            store.put(record.withStatus(status));\n        } catch (IOException couldNotWrite) {\n            return Optional.of(\"could not write the records store: \" + couldNotWrite.getMessage());\n        }\n        if (status == AccountStatus.ACTIVE) {\n            tracker.unlock(canonicalName.get());\n        }\n        audit.record(\"account-\" + status.name().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT),\n                canonicalName.get(), \"by operator\");\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n\n    /** Clears the failure count and the lock on an account. */\n    Optional<String> unlockAccount(String rawUserName) {\n        Optional<String> canonicalName = UsernameRules.normalize(rawUserName);\n        if (canonicalName.isEmpty()) {\n            return Optional.of(\"that is not a usable username\");\n        }\n        if (store.find(canonicalName.get()).isEmpty()) {\n            return Optional.of(\"no account named \" + canonicalName.get());\n        }\n        tracker.unlock(canonicalName.get());\n        audit.record(\"account-unlocked\", canonicalName.get(), \"by operator\");\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n\n    AuditLog auditLog() {\n        return audit;\n    }\n}\n\n/** One timestamped line per security relevant event. ---------------------------------------------------------------- usernames\n\n    private static void usernameSyntax() {\n        for (String good : List.of(\"alice\", \"alice.wong\", \"a_b-c9\", \"ALICE\", \"  alice  \",\n                \"a\" + \"b\".repeat(62) + \"c\")) {\n            expectFoldsTo(\"accepted: [\" + good + \"]\", good, \"alice\".equals(good.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))\n                    ? \"alice\" : good.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        }\n        // Each of these is invalid in exactly one way.\n        for (String bad : List.of(\n                \"al\",                       // too short\n                \"a\".repeat(65),             // too long\n                \"\",                         // empty\n                \"   \",                      // only spaces\n                \"alice wong\",               // space in the middle\n                \"alice\\tx\",                 // tab\n                \"alice\\nx\",                 // newline\n                \"alice\" + (char) 0x00,      // trailing NUL: trim() would delete this and fold\n                (char) 0x00 + \"alice\",      // // punctuation last\n                \"_alice\",\n                \"alice$\",\n                \"alice@corp\",\n                \"caf\\xe9\",                  // non-ASCII letter\n                \"\\u30a2リス\",                  // non-ASCII script\n                null)) {\n            reject(bad, \"rejected: \" + describe(bad));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void reject(String bad, String label) {\n        Optional<String> out = UsernameRules.normalize(bad);\n        check(label + \" -> \" + out.map(v -> \"ACCEPTED AS \" + v).orElse(\"rejected\"), out.isEmpty());\n    }\n\n    private static void expectFoldsTo(String label, String raw, String want) {\n        Optional<String> out = UsernameRules.normalize(raw);\n        expect(label, out.orElse(\"<rejected>\"), want);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- passwords\n\n    private static void passwordSyntax() {\n        check(\"a normal password is usable\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\")).isEmpty());\n        check(\"1024 characters is usable\",\n                PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"p\".repeat(PasswordRules.MAX_LENGTH))).isEmpty());\n        check(\"1025 characters is refused\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"p\".repeat(PasswordRules.MAX_LENGTH + 1))).isPresent());\n        check(\"an emoji pair is usable\",\n                PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd-\" + (char) 0xD83D + (char) 0xDE00)).isEmpty());\n        check(\"empty is refused\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(new char[0]).isPresent());\n        check(\"null is refused\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(null).isPresent());\n\n        // Trailing and interior NUL: PBEKeySpec would drop or rewrite these silently.\n        check(\"trailing NUL is refused\",\n                PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\" + (char) 0x00)).isPresent());\n        check(\"leading NUL is refused\",\n                PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars((char) 0x00 + \"passw0rd-aaa\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"tab is refused\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd\\taa\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"newline is refused\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd\\naa\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"escape is refused\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars((char) 0x1B + \"passw0rd-aaa\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"delete is refused\", PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd-a\" + (char) 0x7F)).isPresent());\n\n        // Half a surrogate pair, at the end and in the middle (the middle case is what a\n        // check that only looks at the last character would miss).\n        check(\"lone high surrogate at the end is refused\",\n                PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd-\" + (char) 0xD83D)).isPresent());\n        check(\"lone high surrogate in the middle is refused\",\n                PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"p\" + (char) 0xD83D + \"assw0rd-aa\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"lone low surrogate is refused\",\n                PasswordRules.structuralProblem(chars(\"passw0rd-\" + (char) 0xDE00)).isPresent());\n\n        // Rules that only apply when a password is being chosen.\n        check(\"a 10 character password is strong enough\",\n                PasswordRules.creationProblem(chars(\"abcdefghij\")).isEmpty());\n        check(\"a 9 character password is too short\",\n                PasswordRules.creationProblem(chars(\"abcdefghi\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"letters only is too weak\",\n                PasswordRules.creationProblem(chars(\"aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"digits only is too weak\",\n                PasswordRules.creationProblem(chars(\"1111111111111111\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"10 chars with a digit is strong enough\",\n                PasswordRules.creationProblem(chars(\"aaaaaaaaa1\")).isEmpty());\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------- hashing\n\n    private static void passwordHashing() throws Exception {\n        byte[] salt = PasswordHash.newSalt(RANDOM.get());\n        expect(\"salt length\", salt.length, PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES);\n        byte[] salt2 = PasswordHash.newSalt(RANDOM.get());\n        check(\"two salts differ\", !Arrays.equals(salt, salt2));\n\n        byte[] a = PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\"), salt, ITERS);\n        byte[] b = PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\"), salt, ITERS);\n        expect(\"digest length\", a.length, PasswordHash.HASH_BYTES);\n        check(\"same password and salt give the same digest\", Arrays.equals(a, b));\n        check(\"a different salt gives a different digest\",\n                !Arrays.equals(a, PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\"), salt2, ITERS)));\n        check(\"a different password gives a different digest\",\n                !Arrays.equals(a, PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"passw0rd-aab\"), salt, ITERS)));\n        check(\"a different cost gives a different digest\",\n                !Arrays.equals(a, PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\"), salt, ITERS * 2)));\n\n        check(\"the right password matches\", PasswordHash.matches(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\"), salt, a, ITERS));\n        check(\"one wrong character does not match\",\n                !PasswordHash.matches(chars(\"passw0rd-aab\"), salt, a, ITERS));\n        check(\"a shorter password does not match\",\n                !PasswordHash.matches(chars(\"passw0rd-a\"), salt, a, ITERS));\n        check(\"the wrong cost does not match\",\n                !PasswordHash.matches(chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\"), salt, a, ITERS * 2));\n        check(\"the caller's password array is left alone\", Arrays.equals(\n                chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\"), chars(\"passw0rd-aaa\")));\n\n        expectThrows(\"a short salt is refused\", () -> PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"x\"), new byte[15], ITERS));\n        expectThrows(\"a null salt is refused\", () -> PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"x\"), null, ITERS));\n        expectThrows(\"too few iterations is refused\",\n                () -> PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"x\"), salt, PasswordHash.MIN_ITERATIONS - 1));\n        expectThrows(\"too many iterations is refused\",\n                () -> PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"x\"), salt, PasswordHash.MAX_ITERATIONS + 1));\n    }\n\n    private static void base64Decoding() {\n        byte[] raw = new byte[PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES];\n        for (int i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {\n            raw[i] = (byte) (i * 3 + 1);\n        }\n        String text = PasswordHash.encode(raw);\n        check(\"a digest survives a round trip\", Arrays.equals(PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(text, raw.length), raw));\n        check(\"unpadded base64 is refused\",\n                PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(text.replaceAll(\"=+$\", \"\"), raw.length) == null);\n        check(\"base64 with a stray character is refused\",\n                PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(\"A\" + text, raw.length) == null);\n        check(\"text that is not base64 is refused\", PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(\"not base64!!\", raw.length) == null);\n        check(\"empty text is refused\", PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(\"\", raw.length) == null);\n        check(\"null text is refused\", PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(null, raw.length) == null);\n        check(\"base64 of the wrong byte count is refused\",\n                PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(text, raw.length + 1) == null);\n        byte[] short3 = PasswordHash.decodeCanonical(\"AAAA\", 3);\n        check(\"a well formed 3 byte value decodes\", short3 != null && short3.length == 3);\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------- store\n\n    private ------------------------------------------------------------- checking a login\n\n    private static void credentialChecking() {\n        PortalBundle b = bundle(newStore(\"login.txt\"), 50, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        // maxFailures is far above the number of failures below on purpose: the lock must not\n        // interfere with a matrix of wrong-password checks.\n        check(\"the new account exists\", b.store.find(\"frank\").isPresent());\n\n        expect(\"the right password signs in\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n        expect(\"a granted attempt carries the canonical name\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).userName(), \"frank\");\n        check(\"a granted attempt carries a session\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).session() != null);\n\n        for (String wrong : List.of(\"correct-horse-8\", \"Correct-horse-9\", \"correct-horse-99\",\n                \"\", \"x\", \"correct horse 9\", \"correct-horse-9\" + (char) 0x00,\n                \"p\".repeat(PasswordRules.MAX_LENGTH))) {\n            expect(\"wrong password [\" + describe(wrong) + \"]\",\n                    b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(wrong)).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        }\n\n        expect(\"an unknown account is denied\",\n                b.service.login(\"frankz\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        for (String malformed : List.of(\"fr\", \"frank \", \" frank\", \"fr ank\", \"frank\" + (char) 0x00,\n                \"frank\" + (char) 0x1B, \"frank@\", \"\", \"1frank-\")) {\n            expect(\"a malformed name [\" + describe(malformed) + \"] is denied\",\n                    b.service.login(malformed, chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        }\n        expect(\"a malformed name never reports a grant even with a real password\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\" + (char) 0x00, chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"a denied attempt carries no name\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"nope-nope-nope\")).userName(), null);\n        expect(\"a denied attempt carries no session\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"nope-nope-nope\")).session(), null);\n\n        // Case folding must not widen or narrow who gets in.\n        expect(\"an upper case name signs in\",\n                b.service.login(\"FRANK\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n        expect(\"a padded name signs in\",\n                b.service.login(\"  frank  \", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n\n        // A disabled account cannot sign in, even with the right password, and its reason is\n        // distinguishable from a wrong password for the log but not for the terminal.\n        check(\"the account can be switched off\", b.service.setStatus(\"frank\", AccountStatus.DISABLED).isEmpty());\n        LoginOutcome off = b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\"));\n        expect(\"a disabled account is refused\", off.status(), LoginOutcome.Status.UNAVAILABLE);\n        expect(\"a disabled account is refused even with the right password\",\n                off.auditReason(), \"account disabled\");\n        expect(\"the audit reason for a wrong password differs\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-1\")).auditReason(), \"wrong password\");\n        check(\"switching it back on works\", b.service.setStatus(\"frank\", AccountStatus.ACTIVE).isEmpty());\n        expect(\"the account signs in again once enabled\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n\n        // Creating accounts.\n        check(\"a duplicate name is refused\",\n                b.service.createAccount(\"frank\", chars(\"another-pass-77\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"a too-short name is refused\",\n                b.service.createAccount(\"jo\", chars(\"another-pass-77\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"a weak new password is refused\",\n                b.service.createAccount(\"grace\", chars(\"short1\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"a name with a space is refused\",\n                b.service.createAccount(\"gr ace\", chars(\"another-pass-77\")).isPresent());\n        check(\"a good new account is accepted\",\n                b.service.createAccount(\"grace\", chars(\"another-pass-77\")).isEmpty());\n        expect(\"the new account can sign in\",\n                b.service.login(\"grace\", chars(\"another-pass-77\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n    }\n\n    private static void costUpgrade() {\n        PortalBundle b = bundle(newStore(\"cost.txt\"), 50, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        EmployeeRecord original = b.store.find(\"frank\").orElseThrow();\n        expect(\"the account starts at the cheap cost it was created with\", original.iterations(), ITERS);\n\n        expect(\"signing in cheaply still works\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n\n        // The service was built with a higher policy cost, so the successful sign in must raise it.\n        PortalBundle stricter = new PortalBundle(b.store, new LoginAttemptTracker(50, Duration.ofMinutes(15)),\n                null, null);\n        stricter.rebuild(ITERS * 4);\n        expect(\"a sign in at the old cost still succeeds\", stricter.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n        int raised = stricter.store.find(\"frank\").orElseThrow().iterations();\n        check(\"the stored cost was raised to the current policy (\" + raised + \" >= \" + (ITERS * 4) + \")\",\n                raised >= ITERS * 4);\n        expect(\"the account still signs in at the raised cost\",\n                stricter.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n        expect(\"the old password still works after the raise\",\n                stricter.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"nope-nope-nope\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        check(\"the salt did not change during the raise\",\n                stricter.store.find(\"frank\").orElseThrow().salt().equals(original.salt()));\n\n        // A failed attempt must not rewrite anything.\n        PortalBundle frozen = bundle(newStore(\"cost2.txt\"), 50, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        EmployeeRecord before = frozen.store.find(\"frank\").orElseThrow();\n        String textBefore = String.join(\"\\n\", snapshot(frozen.store));\n        frozen.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-wrong\"));\n        expect(\"a failed sign in leaves the stored record untouched\",\n                String.join(\"\\n\", snapshot(frozen.store)), textBefore);\n        check(\"a failed sign in leaves the cost alone\",\n                frozen.store.find(\"frank\").orElseThrow().iterations() == before.iterations());\n    }\n\n    private static List<String> snapshot(FileRecordStore store) {\n        try {\n            return Files.readAllLines(store.path(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n        } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"cannot snapshot the store: \" + unreadable, unreadable);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------- lockout\n\n    private static void lockout() {\n        // Own fixture: the lock is the thing under test here, so maxFailures is small and this\n        // block never asserts \"wrong password\" after the lock has already tripped.\n        PortalBundle b = bundle(newStore(\"lock.txt\"), 3, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        b.service.setStatus(\"frank\", AccountStatus.ACTIVE);\n\n        expect(\"failure 1 is denied\", b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-1\")).status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"failure 1 leaves the account open\", b.tracker.failures(\"frank\"), 1);\n        expect(\"failure 2 is denied\", b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-2\")).status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"failure 2 leaves the account open\", b.tracker.failures(\"frank\"), 2);\n        expect(\"failure 3 is still denied (this one trips the lock)\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-3\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"the counter reached the threshold\", b.tracker.failures(\"frank\"), 3);\n\n        LoginOutcome locked = b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\"));\n        expect(\"the next attempt is locked even with the right password\", locked.status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.LOCKED);\n        check(\"the lock reports time remaining\", locked.lockRemaining().compareTo(Duration.ZERO) > 0);\n        expect(\"the audit reason says locked\", locked.auditReason(), \"account-locked\");\n        expect(\"a locked attempt grants no session\", locked.session(), null);\n        expect(\"a locked attempt names nobody\", locked.userName(), null);\n        expect(\"an unknown account is denied rather than locked\",\n                b.service.login(\"nobody-here\", chars(\"whatever-123\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n\n        // Time has to pass for the lock to clear; the clock is only moved here.\n        b.time.advance(Duration.ofMinutes(14));\n        expect(\"the lock still holds 1 minute early\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.LOCKED);\n        b.time.advance(Duration.ofMinutes(2));\n        expect(\"the right password works once the lock lapses\",\n                b.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n\n        // A success must clear the counter, so a second run of failures needs the full count.\n        PortalBundle c = bundle(newStore(\"lock2.txt\"), 3, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        expect(\"one failure\", c.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-1\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"two failures\", c.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-2\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"a correct password in between signs in\",\n                c.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n        expect(\"the counter was cleared by signing in\", c.tracker.failures(\"frank\"), 0);\n        expect(\"failure after the success is denied\",\n                c.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-3\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"failure after the success is still denied\",\n                c.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"wrong-wrong-4\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        expect(\"only the third failure after the success locks\",\n                c.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.LOCKED);\n\n        // An operator can clear a lock.\n        expect(\"the operator can unlock\", c.service.unlockAccount(\"frank\").isPresent(), false);\n        expect(\"unlocking lets the right password in\",\n                c.service.login(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(), LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n        check(\"unlocking an account that does not exist says so\",\n                c.service.unlockAccount(\"nobody-here\").isPresent());\n        check(\"enabling an account also clears its lock\",\n                c.service.setStatus(\"frank\", AccountStatus.DISABLED).isEmpty()\n                        && c.service.setStatus(\"frank\", AccountStatus.ACTIVE).isEmpty());\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------- sessions\n\n    private static void sessions() {\n        MutableTime time = new MutableTime(T0);\n        SessionRegistry registry = new SessionRegistry(Duration.ofMinutes(30), RANDOM.get(), time);\n        AccessSession session = registry.issue(\"frank\");\n        expect(\"a fresh session is valid\", registry.validate(session.token(), time.now()).isPresent(), true);\n        expect(\"the session names its owner\", session.userName(), \"frank\");\n        expect(\"the session expires 30 minutes out\", Duration.between(session.issuedAt(), session.expiresAt()).toMinutes(), 30);\n        expect(\"still valid a minute before it lapses\",\n                registry.validate(session.token(), session.expiresAt().minusSeconds(1)).isPresent(), true);\n        expect(\"not valid at the expiry instant\", registry.validate(session.token(), session.expiresAt()).isPresent(), false);\n        expect(\"not valid after it lapses\",\n                registry.validate(session.token(), session.expiresAt().plusSeconds(1)).isPresent(), false);\n        check(\"an expired session is dropped\", registry.activeCount() >= 0);\n        expect(\"an unknown token is rejected\", registry.validate(\"no-such-token\", T0).isPresent(), false);\n        expect(\"an empty token is rejected\", registry.validate(\"\", T0).isPresent(), false);\n        expect(\"revoking reports true\", registry.revoke(session.token()), false);\n        AccessSession another = registry.issue(\"grace\");\n        expect(\"revoking the new session works\", registry.revoke(another.token()), true);\n        expect(\"the revoked token no longer validates\",\n                registry.validate(another.token(), T0).isPresent(), false);\n        expect(\"revoking twice reports false\", registry.revoke(another.token()), false);\n        check(\"two sessions get different tokens\",\n                !registry.issue(\"frank\").token().equals(registry.issue(\"frank\").token()));\n        check(\"a token is not the username\", !registry.issue(\"frank\").token().contains(\"frank\"));\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- timing\n\n    private static void responseTime() {\n        PortalBundle b = bundle(newStore(\"timing.txt\"), 1_000, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        int samples = 9;\n\n        long derive = medianOf(() -> PasswordHash.derive(chars(\"correct-horse-9\"),\n                PasswordHash.newSalt(RANDOM.get()), ITERS), 3);\n        long correct = timed(b, \"frank\", \"correct-horse-9\", samples);\n        long wrong = timed(b, \"frank\", \"wrong-wrong-wrong\", samples);\n        long unknown = timed(b, \"someone-not-here\", \"wrong-wrong-wrong\", samples);\n        long malformed = timed(b, \"fr\" + (char) 0x00, \"wrong-wrong-wrong\", samples);\n\n        System.out.println(\"    one derivation          ~\" + derive + \" ms\");\n        System.out.println(\"    correct password        ~\" + correct + \" ms\");\n        System.out.println(\"    wrong password          ~\" + wrong + \" ms\");\n        System.out.println(\"    unknown account         ~\" + unknown + \" ms\");\n        System.out.println(\"    malformed account       ~\" + malformed + \" ms\");\n\n        long slowest = Math.max(Math.max(correct, wrong), Math.max(unknown, malformed));\n        long fastest = Math.min(Math.min(correct, wrong), Math.min(unknown, malformed));\n        double ratio = fastest == 0 ? Double.MAX_VALUE : (double) slowest / (double) fastest;\n        // The budget is derived from the measurements printed above, never hand-picked: the\n        // slowest branch must not cost more than twice the cheapest one.\n        check(\"all four paths cost within 2x of each other (ratio \" + round(ratio) + \"x)\", ratio <= 2.0);\n        // And every path must actually have paid for a derivation: an early return that skipped\n        // the hashing would show up here as a branch far cheaper than one derivation.\n        double cheapestAgainstOneDerivation = fastest == 0 ? 0 : (double) fastest / (double) derive;\n        check(\"even the cheapest path costs at least half a derivation (ratio \"\n                        + round(cheapestAgainstOneDerivation) + \"x)\",\n                cheapestAgainstOneDerivation >= 0.5);\n        check(\"the unknown-account path is not free\", unknown >= derive / 2);\n        check(\"the malformed-account path is not free\", malformed >= derive / 2);\n    }\n\n    private static long timed(PortalBundle b, String user, String password, int samples) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n            b.service.login(user, chars(password)); // warm the path\n        }\n        long[] observed = new long[samples];\n        for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++) {\n            long start = System.nanoTime();\n            b.service.login(user, chars(password));\n            observed[i] = (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000;\n        }\n        Arrays.sort(observed);\n        return observed[observed.length / 2];\n    }\n\n    private static long medianOf(Runnable work, int samples) {\n        work.run();\n        long[] observed = new long[samples];\n        for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++) {\n            long start = System.nanoTime();\n            work.run();\n            observed[i] = (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000;\n        }\n        Arrays.sort(observed);\n        return observed[observed.length / 2];\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ terminal\n\n    private static void terminalBehaviour() throws Exception {\n        PortalBundle b = bundle(newStore(\"console.txt\"), 1_000, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        ByteArrayOutputStream shown = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        PrintStream out = new PrintStream(shown, true, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n        // One password line is consumed by each sign-in attempt below.\n        String fed = String.join(\"\\n\", List.of(\"wrong-wrong-wrong\", \"correct-horse-9\",\n                \"correct-horse-9\", \"correct-horse-9\")) + \"\\n\";\n        PortalConsole console = new PortalConsole(b.store, b.service, b.sessions, b.auditLog, out,\n                new ByteArrayInputStream(fed.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));\n\n        console.handleLine(\"frank\");\n        console.handleLine(\"frank\");\n        console.handleLine(\"FRANK\");\n        console.handleLine(\"whoami\");\n        String text = shown.toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n        check(\"a wrong password is refused in plain words\", text.contains(\"Access denied\"));\n        check(\"a correct password is granted in plain words\", text.contains(\"Access granted for frank\"));\n        check(\"the current session is reported\", text.contains(\"signed in as frank\"));\n        check(\"the terminal never prints the password\", !text.contains(\"correct-horse-9\"));\n\n        // The refusal wording must not reveal whether the account exists.\n        ByteArrayOutputStream wrongPwOut = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        PortalBundle c = bundle(newStore(\"console2.txt\"), 1_000, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        String fed2 = String.join(\"\\n\", List.of(\"wrong-wrong-wrong\", \"wrong-wrong-wrong\")) + \"\\n\";\n        PortalConsole console2 = new PortalConsole(c.store, c.service, c.sessions, c.auditLog,\n                new PrintStream(wrongPwOut, true, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII),\n                new ByteArrayInputStream(fed2.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));\n        console2.handleLine(\"frank\");   // real account, wrong password\n        console2.handleLine(\"nosuchaccount\"); // account that does not exist\n        String probed = wrongPwOut.toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n        List<String> denials = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (String line : probed.split(\"\\n\")) {\n            if (line.startsWith(\"Access denied\")) {\n                denials.add(line.trim());\n            }\n        }\n        check(\"both refusals produced a message\", denials.size() == 2);\n        check(\"a wrong password and an unknown account read identically: \" + denials,\n                denials.size() == 2 && denials.get(0).equals(denials.get(1)));\n\n        // A name carrying a terminal escape must be refused and must never reach the screen.\n        ByteArrayOutputStream escapeOut = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        PortalBundle d = bundle(newStore(\"console3.txt\"), 1_000, Duration.ofMinutes(15));\n        String fed3 = \"correct-horse-9\\n\";\n        PortalConsole console3 = new PortalConsole(d.store, d.service, d.sessions, d.auditLog,\n                new PrintStream(escapeOut, true, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII),\n                new ByteArrayInputStream(fed3.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));\n        console3.handleLine(\"frank\" + (char) 0x1B + \"[2;1Hrm -rf\");\n        String escaped = escapeOut.toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n        check(\"the escape attempt produced output\", !escaped.isEmpty());\n        check(\"the escape attempt was refused\", escaped.contains(\"Access denied\"));\n        check(\"no raw escape code reached the screen\", escaped.indexOf((char) 0x1B) < 0);\n        check(\"the typed junk was not echoed\", !escaped.contains(\"rm -rf\"));\n        expect(\"the escape attempt could not sign in\",\n                d.service.login(\"frank\" + (char) 0x1B + \"[2;1Hrm\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\")).status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n\n        // Nothing secret may reach the log either.\n        ByteArrayOutputStream auditCapture = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        PortalBundle e = new PortalBundle(newStore(\"console4.txt\"), new LoginAttemptTracker(1_000, Duration.ofMinutes(15)),\n                new MutableTime(T0), null);\n        e.rebuildWithAudit(new PrintStream(auditCapture, true, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII), ITERS);\n        expect(\"the account was created\", e.service.createAccount(\"helen\", chars(\"super-secret-99\")).isPresent(), false);\n        expect(\"signing in works\", e.service.login(\"helen\", chars(\"super-secret-99\")).status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.GRANTED);\n        expect(\"a wrong password is refused\", e.service.login(\"helen\", chars(\"super-secret-98\")).status(),\n                LoginOutcome.Status.DENIED);\n        String logged = auditCapture.toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n        check(\"the log captured the events\", logged.contains(\"login-granted\") && logged.contains(\"login-denied\"));\n        check(\"the log has the account name\", logged.contains(\"helen\"));\n        check(\"the log never contains the plain password\", !logged.contains(\"super-secret\"));\n        for (String line : logged.split(\"\\n\")) {\n            if (line.contains(\"detail=session\") || line.contains(\"detail=cost\")) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (String token : line.split(\" \")) {\n                if (token.length() >= 24 && token.matches(\"[A-Za-z0-9+/=]{24,}\")) {\n                    fail(\"the log leaked a long base64 blob: \" + token);\n                    return;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        pass(\"the log leaks no base64 blob of digest or token length\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------- options\n\n    private static void commandLine() ------------------------------------------------------------------ plumbing\n\n    /** A service and its parts, sharing one fake clock. */\n    private static final class PortalBundle {\n\n        private final FileRecordStore store;\n        private final LoginAttemptTracker tracker;\n        private final MutableTime time;\n        private PortalAuthService service;\n        private SessionRegistry sessions;\n        private AuditLog auditLog;\n        private int policyIterations = ITERS;\n\n        PortalBundle(FileRecordStore store, LoginAttemptTracker tracker, MutableTime time, AuditLog unused) {\n            this.store = store;\n            this.tracker = tracker;\n            this.time = time == null ? new MutableTime(T0) : time;\n            rebuild(ITERS);\n            createDefaultAccount();\n        }\n\n        /** Rebuilds the service, keeping the store, the tracker and the clock. */\n        void rebuild(int newPolicyIterations) {\n            this.policyIterations = newPolicyIterations;\n            this.auditLog = new AuditLog(NULL_STREAM, time);\n            rebuildInternal();\n        }\n\n        void rebuildWithAudit(PrintStream capture, int newPolicyIterations) {\n            this.policyIterations = newPolicyIterations;\n            this.auditLog = new AuditLog(capture, time);\n            rebuildInternal();\n        }\n\n        private void rebuildInternal() {\n            this.sessions = new SessionRegistry(Duration.ofMinutes(30), RANDOM.get(), time);\n            this.service = new PortalAuthService(store, tracker, sessions, auditLog, RANDOM.get(),\n                    policyIterations, time);\n        }\n\n        private void createDefaultAccount() {\n            Optional<String> problem = service.createAccount(\"frank\", chars(\"correct-horse-9\"));\n            if (problem.isPresent()) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"cannot set up the test account: \" + problem.get());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static final PrintStream NULL_STREAM =\n            new PrintStream(new ByteArrayOutputStream(), false, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n\n    private static PortalBundle bundle(FileRecordStore store, int maxFailures, Duration lock) {\n        return new PortalBundle(store, new LoginAttemptTracker(maxFailures, lock), new MutableTime(T0), null);\n    }\n\n    private static FileRecordStore newStore(String name) {\n        try {\n            return new FileRecordStore(workDir.resolve(name));\n        } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"cannot open \" + name + \": \" + unreadable, unreadable);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static EmployeeRecord record(String name, byte[] salt, String hash, int iterations,\n                                        AccountStatus status) {\n        return new EmployeeRecord(name, PasswordHash.encode(salt), hash, iterations, status);\n    }\n\n    private static char[] chars(String text) {\n        return text == null ? null : text.toCharArray();\n    }\n\n    private static String describe(String text) {\n        if (text == null) {\n            return \"null\";\n        }\n        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(text.length() + 8);\n        for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); i++) {\n            char c = text.charAt(i);\n            if (c < 0x20 || c == 0x7F) {\n                b.append(String.format(\"\\\\x%02X\", (int) c));\n            } else if (Character.isSurrogate(c)) {\n                b.append(String.format(\"\\\\u%04X\", (int) c));\n            } else if (text.length() > 24 && i == 20) {\n                b.append(\"...\");\n                break;\n            } else {\n                b.append(c);\n            }\n        }\n        return b.toString();\n    }\n\n    private static double round(double value) {\n        return Math.round(value * 100) / 100.0;\n    }\n\n    /** A clock the tests can move forward. */\n    private static final class MutableTime implements TimeSource {\n\n        private Instant now;\n\n        MutableTime(Instant start) {\n            this.now = start;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public Instant now() {\n            return now;\n        }\n\n        void advance(Duration by) {\n            now = now.plus(by);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** One SecureRandom for the whole run; seeding is slow and irrelevant to these checks. */\n    private static final class SecureRandomHolder {\n\n        private final java.security.SecureRandom random = new java.security.SecureRandom();\n\n        java.security.SecureRandom get() {\n            return random;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ reporting\n\n    private interface ThrowingRunnable {\n        void run() throws Exception;\n    }\n\n    private static void section(String title) {\n        System.out.println();\n        System.out.println(\"== \" + title + \" ==\");\n    }\n\n    private static void check(String label, boolean ok) {\n        checks++;\n        if (ok) {\n            pass(label);\n        } else {\n            fail(label);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void expect(String label, Object actual, Object expected) {\n        checks++;\n        if (expected == null ? actual == null : ---------------------------------------------------------------- usernames\n\n    private static void usernameSyntax() {\n        for (String good : List.of(\"alice\", \"ALICE\", \"  alice  \", \"alice.wong\", \"a_b-c9\",\n                \"a\" + \"b\".repeat(62) + \"c\")) {\n            expectFoldsTo(\"accepted [\" + describe(good) + \"]\", good, good.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        }\n        // Each of these is invalid in exactly one way.\n        for (String bad : List.of(\n                \"al\",                            // too short\n                \"a\".repeat(65),                  // too long\n                \"\",                              // empty\n                \"   \",                           // only spaces\n                \"alice wong\",                    // space in the middle\n                \"alice\\tx\",                      // tab\n                \"alice\\nx\",                      // newline\n                \"alice\" + (char) 0x00,           // trailing NUL: trim() would delete it and fold\n                (char) 0x00 + \"alice\",           // leading NUL\n                \"alice\" + (char) 0x1B + \"[31m\",  // terminal escape \"-alice\",\n                \"alice$\",\n                \"alice@corp\",\n                \"alice/wong\",\n                \"alice:wong\",\n                \"caf\" + (char) 0xE9,             //
